שתי אשמות – that if he had been aware that he had sinned after he brought the suspensive guilt-offering, he then needs to bring a definitive guilt-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:2:5)


**יָבִיא מְעִילָה וְחֻמְשָׁהּ.** קֶרֶן הַמָּמוֹן שֶׁנִּסְתַּפֵּק לוֹ אִם נֶהֱנָה מִמֶּנּוּ וְחֻמְשׁוֹ:


יביא מעילה וחומשה – the principal of the money when it was doubtful to him if he had benefited from it and his added fifth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:2:6)


**בִּשְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים.** אַיִל הַנִּקָּח בִּשְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים, דִּכְתִיב (שם ח) בְּעֶרְכְּךָ כֶּסֶף שְׁקָלִים:


בשתי סלעים – a ram that is purchased for two Selaim (see Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 10, Mishnah 5), as it is written (Leviticus 5:15): “[When a person commits a trespass , being unwittingly remiss about anu of the LORD’s sacred things, he shall bring as his penalty to the LORD a ram without blemish from the flock,] convertible into payment in silver [by the sanctuary weight, as a reparation (i.e., guilt) offering].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:2:7)


**וְאִם סָפֵק.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, וְאִם בִּסְפֵקוֹ עוֹמֵד לְעוֹלָם יִהְיֶה בְּאָשָׁם תָּלוּי:


ואם ספק – this is what he said, but if in his doubt he always stands, it will be a suspensive guilt-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:2:8)


**שֶׁמִּמִּין שֶׁמֵּבִיא עַל הוֹדַע מֵבִיא עַל לֹא הוֹדַע.** הִלְכָּךְ יָכוֹל לְהָבִיא וּלְהַתְנוֹת. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


שממין שמביא על הודע מביא על לא הודע – therefore, he is able to bring and to benefit. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:3:1)


**חַטַּאת הָעוֹף סָפֵק.** הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁיָּלְדָה סְפֵק מִין פְּטוּר סְפֵק מִין חִיּוּב, מְבִיאָה כֶּבֶשׂ לְעוֹלָה, וּמַתְנָה, אִם מִין חִיּוּב יְהֵא לְחוֹבָה, וְאִם מִין פְּטוּר יְהֵא לִנְדָבָה. אֲבָל חַטָּאת מְבִיאָה בְּסָפֵק וְאֵינָהּ נֶאֱכֶלֶת, שֶׁמָּא חֻלִּין הִיא וּמְלִיקַת חֻלִּין נְבֵלָה:


חטאת העוף ספק – a woman who doubtfully gave birth to a species doubtfully exempt (i.e., as to the character or viability of the fetus that was aborted) or that is doubtfully liable [for a sin-offering] , she brings a lamb for a burnt-offering and a gift , if it is species that is liable, it will be for the obligatory offering, and if it is for a species that is exempt, it iwill be for a donation. But the sin-offering that she brings in a doubtful case but it is not consumed, lest it is really unconsecrated and the pinching of the bird’s neck (with one’s fingernail) if something that is unconsecrated is a carrion


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:3:2)


**תַּעֲשֶׂנָּה וַדַּאי.** וְתֵאָכֵל לַכֹּהֲנִים כִּשְׁאָר חַטָּאוֹת:


תעשנה ודאין – that it should be fed to the Kohanim like other sin-offerings (if the fetus is alive).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:3:3)


**שֶׁמִּמִּין שֶׁהִיא מְבִיאָה.** שֶׁהֲרֵי פְּרִידָה אַחַת מִן הַתּוֹרִים אוֹ מִן בְּנֵי הַיּוֹנָה הִיא מְבִיאָה לְחַטָּאת, בֵּין וַדַּאי יוֹלֶדֶת בֵּין עַל סָפֵק יוֹלֶדֶת:


שממין שהיא מביאה – for one of the pairs from the turtle-doves or from one of the pigeons is what she brings as a sin-offering, whether she was a definitive mother of a child or a doubtful mother of a child.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:4:1)


**וְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְחַיֵּב.** רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא לְטַעֲמֵיהּ, דִּמְחַיֵּב אָשָׁם תָּלוּי עַל סְפֵק מְעִילוֹת:


ור' עקיבא מחייב – Rabbi Akiva, according to his reasoning, that requires a suspending reparation/guilt sacrifice on doubtful benefit from religious sacrilege.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:4:2)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם מְבִיאִין אָשָׁם אֶחָד.** בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת, וְיֹאמַר זֶה לָזֶה, אִם אַתָּה אָכַלְתָּ הַחֲתִיכָה שֶׁל קֹדֶשׁ יְהֵא חֶלְקִי מָחוּל לְךָ וִיהֵא אָשָׁם כֻּלּוֹ עָלֶיךָ:


שניהם מביאין אשם אחד – in partnership, and each one says to the other, “If you ae the piece of meat of Holy Things, may my portion be forgiven to you, and may the guilt offering be completely upon you.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:4:3)


**אֵין שְׁנַיִם מְבִיאִים אָשָׁם אֶחָד.** דְּלֵית לֵיהּ תְּנַאי בַּקָּרְבָּנוֹת, אֶלָּא אִם כְּדִבְרֵי חֲכָמִים שְׁנֵיהֶם פְּטוּרִים, אִי כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא כָּל אֶחָד מֵבִיא אָשָׁם תָּלוּי. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים שֶׁאֵין מְבִיאִים אָשָׁם תָּלוּי עַל סְפֵק מְעִילוֹת:


אין שנים מביאים אשם אחד – for he (i.e., Rabbi Yosi) does not hold he condition for sacrifices, but if it is according to the words of the Sages, both of them are exempt; if it is according to Rabbi Akiva, each one brings a suspensive guilt offering. But the Halakha is according to the Sages, for one does not bring a suspensive guilt offering on doubtful religious sacrilege.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:5:1)


**חַטָּאת אֶחָת.** וּמַתְנֶה זֶה עִם זֶה כִּדְפָרֵישְׁנָא לְעֵיל:


חטאה אחת – and they make a condition one with the other, as it is explained above (Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:5:2)


**אֵין שְׁנַיִם מְבִיאִין חַטָּאת אֶחָת.** אֲבָל אָשָׁם תָּלוּי מַיְתֵי כָּל חַד וְחַד, מִשּׁוּם חֲתִיכַת הַחֵלֶב. וְהַיְנוּ תַּנָּא קַמָּא. וְהָא קָמַשְׁמַע לָן דְּתַנָּא קַמָּא רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הוּא, וַהֲלָכָה כְּמוֹתוֹ:


ואין שנים מביאין חטאת אחת – but each one of them brings a suspensive guilt offering, because of the piece of [meat containing] forbidden fat, and that is equivalent to the first Tanna/teacher. But this comes to tell us, that the first Tanna is Rabbi Yossi, and the Halakha is according to him.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:6:1)


**חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל חֵלֶב וַחֲתִיכָה שֶׁל קֹדֶשׁ כוּ'‏ מֵבִיא אָשָׁם תָּלוּי.** אֲפִלּוּ לְרַבָּנָן דְּאָמְרֵי אֵין מְבִיאִים אָשָׁם תָּלוּי עַל סְפֵק מְעִילוֹת, הָכָא מוֹדוּ דְּחַיָּב, מִשּׁוּם חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל חֵלֶב:


חתיכה של חלב חתיכה של קודש כו' מביא אשם תלוי – even according to the Rabbis who stat that they don’t bring a suspensive guilt-offering on doubtful religious sacrileges, here they admit that he is liable, because of the piece of [meat containing] forbidden fat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:6:2)


**מֵבִיא חַטָּאת וְאָשָׁם וַדָּאי.** עַל הַחֲתִיכָה שֶׁל חֵלֶב מֵבִיא חַטָּאת, וְעַל בָּשָׂר שֶׁל קֹדֶשׁ אָשָׁם וַדַּאי:


מביא חטאת ואשם ודאי – on the piece of forbidden fat, he brings a sin offering, and on the meat of Holy Things, he brings an unconditional guilt-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:6:3)


**חַטָּאת וְאָשָׁם.** בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת. וּמַתְנֶה, אִם אֲנִי אָכַלְתִּי חֵלֶב וְאַתָּה קֹדֶשׁ יְהֵא חֶלְקִי בָּאָשָׁם מָחוּל לְךָ וְחֶלְקְךָ בַּחַטָּאת מָחוּל לִי. וְאִם אֲנִי אָכַלְתִּי קֹדֶשׁ וְאַתָּה חֵלֶב יְהֵא חֶלְקִי בַּחַטָּאת מָחוּל לְךָ וְחֶלְקְךָ בָּאָשָׁם מָחוּל לִי:


חטאת ואשם – in partnership, and makes a condition: “if I ate forbidden fat, and you – Holy Things, may my part with a guilt-offering be forgiven to you, and your part with a sin-offering be forgiven to me, but if I consumed Holy Things and you – forbidden fat, may my part with a sin-offering be forgiven to you, and your part with a guilt offering be forgiven to me.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:6:4)


**אֵין שְׁנַיִם מְבִיאִים חַטָּאת וְאָשָׁם.** בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת, אֶלָּא זֶה מֵבִיא אָשָׁם תָּלוּי וְזֶה מֵבִיא אָשָׁם תָּלוּי. וְהַיְנוּ תַּנָּא קַמָּא. וְהָא קָמַשְׁמַע לָן דְּתַנָּא קַמָּא הוּא רַבִּי יוֹסֵי וַהֲלָכָה כְּמוֹתוֹ:


אין שנים מביאים חטאת ואשם – in partnership, but rather, this one brings a suspensive guilt-offering and that one brings a suspensive guilt offering. But this is [the opinion of] the first Tanna/teacher. But this comes to teach us that the first Tanna/teacher is Rabbi Yossi, and the Halakha is according to him.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:1)


**חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל חֵלֶב וַחֲתִיכָה שֶׁל חֵלֶב קֹדֶשׁ כוּ'‏ מֵבִיא חַטָּאת.** דְּמִמַּה נַּפְשָׁךְ חֵלֶב אָכַל:


חתיכה של חלב וחתיכה של חלב קודש כו' מביא חטאת – for no matter whichever way your turn, he ate forbidden fat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:2)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר.** אַף אָשָׁם תָּלוּי כַּחַטָּאת (ולשון רש"י עם החטאת), מִשּׁוּם סְפֵק מְעִילוֹת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


ר' עקיבא אומר – even a suspensive guilt-offering is like a sin-offering, because of doubtful religious sacrileges. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Akiva.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:3)


**מֵבִיא שְׁתֵּי חַטָּאוֹת.** וְהוּא שֶׁאֲכָלָן בִּשְׁתֵּי הֶעְלֵמוֹת. שֶׁאִם לֹא הָיְתָה שָׁם יְדִיעָה בֵּינְתַיִם, אֵינוֹ חַיָּב אֶלָּא אַחַת:


מביא שתי חטאות – and he, who ate them in two acts of forgetfulness, for it there had not been there a knowledge/awareness in-between, would only be liable for one [sin-offering].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:4)


**זֶה מֵבִיא אָשָׁם תָּלוּי וְכוּ'.** מוּסָף עַל הַחַטָּאת שֶׁהוּא צָרִיךְ לְהָבִיא מִשּׁוּם אוֹכֵל חֵלֶב מֵבִיא אָשָׁם תָּלוּי מִשּׁוּם סְפֵק מְעִילוֹת:


זה מביא אשם שתוי וכו' – in addition to the sin-offering that he must bring because of eating forbidden fat, he brings a suspensive guilt-offering because of doubtful religious sacrileges.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:5)


**וּשְׁנֵיהֶם מְבִיאִין אָשָׁם אֶחָד.** בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת, וּמַתְנִין:


ושניהם מביאין אשם אחד – in partnership and they make conditions.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:7:6)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְתַנָּא קַמָּא רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הוּא וַהֲלָכָה כְּמוֹתוֹ:


רבי יוסי אומר כו' – the first Tanna/teacher is Rabbi Yossi and the Halakha is according to him.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:8:1)


**חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל חֵלֶב וַחֲתִיכָה שֶׁל חֵלֶב נוֹתָר כוּ'.** מֵבִיא חַטָּאת מִשּׁוּם חֵלֶב, וְאָשָׁם תָּלוּי מֵחֲמַת סָפֵק שֶׁל נוֹתָר, שֶׁהַנּוֹתָר בְּכָרֵת כַּחֵלֶב. וְאִסּוּר נוֹתָר חָל עַל אִסּוּר חֵלֶב לְפִי שֶׁהוּא אִסּוּר מוֹסִיף:


חתיכה של חלב וחתיכה של חלב נותר כו' – He brings a sin-offering because of forbidden fat, and a suspending guilt-offering because of doubt of left-overs, for left-overs [are punishable] by extirpation like that of forbidden fat. But the prohibition of left-overs takes effect on the prohibition of forbidden fat because it is prohibition that adds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:8:2)


**מֵבִיא שָׁלֹשׁ חַטָּאוֹת.** שְׁתַּיִם מִשּׁוּם חֵלֶב וְאַחַת מִשּׁוּם נוֹתָר. וְהוּא שֶׁהָיְתָה לוֹ יְדִיעָה בֵּינְתַיִם, דְּאִי לָאו הָכִי אֵינוֹ מֵבִיא אֶלָּא שְׁתֵּי חַטָּאוֹת, אַחַת מִשּׁוּם חֵלֶב וְאַחַת מִשּׁוּם נוֹתָר. וְהַאי דְּלֹא תָּנֵי הָכָא שָׁלֹשׁ חַטָּאוֹת וְאָשָׁם וַדַּאי כִּדְתָנֵי לְעֵיל, מִשּׁוּם דְּרֹב נוֹתָר אֵין בּוֹ שָׁוֶה פְּרוּטָה, דְּלֹא חֲזִי לְמִידִי, וְאָשָׁם מְעִילוֹת אֵינוֹ בָּא עַל פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁוֶה פְּרוּטָה:


מביא שלש חטאות – two [sin-offerings] because of [eating] forbidden fat, and one because of [consuming] left-overs, and this is when there for him of knowledge/awareness [of his sinning] in the meanwhile, for if not, this one only brings two sin-offerings, one because of [partaking of] forbidden fat and the other because of [eating] left-overs. But one who does not teach here “three sin-offering and an unconditional guilt offering, as is taught above (see Mishnah 6 of this chapter), because most of the left-overs do not contain the equivalent of a penny, and are not worthy of anything, but the guilt-offerings of religious sacrilege do not come on less than the equivalent of a penny.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/5:8:3)


**כָּל חַטָּאת שֶׁהִיא בָאָה עַל חֵטְא.** לַאֲפוֹקֵי חַטַּאת יוֹלֶדֶת שֶׁאֵינָהּ בָּאָה עַל חֵטְא. דְּסָבַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי שֶׁשְּׁתַּיִם מְבִיאוֹת אוֹתָהּ בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת וּבִתְנַאי, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא. וּפְסַק הֲלָכָה, שֶׁאֵין חַטָּאת בָּאָה בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת, וַאֲפִלּוּ חַטָּאת שֶׁל מְחֻסְּרֵי כַּפָּרָה:


כל חטאת שהיא באה על חטא – to exclude the sin-offering of a mother who gave birth which does not for a sin, for Rabbi Yossi holds that two bring it in partnership and with a condition, as we stated in the first chapter. But the Halakhic decision is that a sin-offering is not brought in partnership, and even for sin offerings of lacking atonement.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:1)


**הַמֵּבִיא אָשָׁם תָּלוּי.** יֵצֵא וְיִרְעֶה בָּעֵדֶר. עִם שְׁאָר צֹאנוֹ, כְּחֻלִּין גְּמוּרִים. דְּסָבַר רַבִּי מֵאִיר, כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא צָרִיךְ לֵיהּ לֹא מַקְדִּישׁ לֵיהּ:


המביא אשם תלוי. יצא ויראה בעדר – with the rest of his [flock of] sheep like completely unconsecrated [animals], For Rabbi Meir holds that since he doesn’t need it, he doesn’t sanctify it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:2)


**יִרְעֶה עַד שֶׁיִּסְתָּאֵב.** דְּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁלִּבּוֹ נוֹקְפוֹ בִּשְׁעַת הַהַפְרָשָׁה וּמִתְיָרֵא מִסְּפֵק הַחֵטְא, גָּמַר וּמַקְדִּישׁ לֵיהּ וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא יִצְטָרֵךְ, הִלְכָּךְ יִרְעֶה עַד שֶׁיִּפֹּל בּוֹ מוּם וְיִפְּלוּ דָּמָיו לִנְדָבָה לְהָבִיא מֵהֶן עוֹלַת נְדָבָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ירעה עד שיסתאב (sent out to pasture until it is made unclean) – but since he may have scruples (i.e., he has no clear conscience) at the time of the separation and he fears from doubtful sin, he resolved and sanctified it, and even though it might not be necessary. Therefore, it is sent out to pasture until a defect befalls it (i.e., the animal) and its monetary value falls to a free-will contribution to bring with them a donation of a burnt-offering. But the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:3)


**הַדָּם יִשָּׁפֵךְ.** לָאַמָּה (שבמקדש) [שֶׁבָּעֲזָרָה]:


הדם ישפך – to the arm-pit (i.e., the name of an opening in the Temple door) that is in the Temple [one needs to say: that is in the Temple courtyard].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:4)


**לְבֵית הַשְּׂרֵפָה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּחֻלִּין שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטוּ בָּעֲזָרָה טְעוּנִים קְבוּרָה, הַאי הָוֵי כְּזֶבַח פָּסוּל שֶׁטָּעוּן שְׂרֵפָה:


לבית השריפה (the place of burning) – and even though that unconsecrated meat that is ritually slaughtered I the Temple courtyard require burial, this is like an disqualified sacrifice that requires burning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:5)


**נִזְרַק הַדָּם.** קֹדֶם שֶׁנּוֹדַע לוֹ:


נזרק הדם – before he became aware [that he had not sinned].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:6)


**יֵאָכֵל.** הַבָּשָׂר. דְּרַחֲמָנָא אָמַר (ויקרא ה) וְהוּא לֹא יָדַע וְנִסְלַח לוֹ, בִּשְׁעַת סְלִיחָה לֹא תְּהֵא יְדִיעָה. וְהָכָא נַמִּי בִּשְׁעַת זְרִיקַת הַדָּם דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁעַת סְלִיחָה לֹא הָיְתָה יְדִיעָה, וַהֲרֵי גָּמַר כָּל כַּפָּרַת סְפֵקוֹ, וַהֲוָה לֵיהּ אָשָׁם תָּלוּי כָּשֵׁר:


יאכל – the flesh/meat is eaten, as the All-Merciful stated (Leviticus 5:18): “[The priest shall make expiation on his behalf for he error that he committed] unwittingly, and he shall be forgiven,” at the time of pardon/ forgiveness, he doesn’t have awareness/knowledge. But here also, at the time of the sprinkling of the blood, that is at the time of pardon/forgiveness, he did not have knowledge, and hence, he completed all the atonement of his doubt, and he should have a kosher suspensive guilt-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:1:7)


**אֲפִלּוּ הַדָּם בַּכּוֹס יִזָּרֵק.** דְּסָבַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת מְקַדְּשִׁין אֶת הַפָּסוּל לִקָּרֵב, וְכָל הָעוֹמֵד לִזָּרֵק כְּזָרוּק דָּמֵי, וְהָוֵי כְּאִלּוּ נִזְרַק הַדָּם כְּבָר קֹדֶם שֶׁהָיְתָה לוֹ יְדִיעָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


אפילו הדם בכוס יזרק – for Rabbi Yossi holds that the service vessels [of the Temple] sanctify the disqualification to be offered, and everything that stands to be sprinkled is considered as if it was sprinkled, and it is as if the blood had already been sprinkled before he had awareness. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:1)


**אָשָׁם וַדַּאי אֵינוֹ כֵן.** בְּהַהִיא לֹא פְּלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן דְּאִם נוֹדַע לוֹ עַד שֶׁלֹּא שְׁחָטוֹ שֶׁלֹּא חָטָא, יֵצֵא וְיִרְעֶה בָּעֵדֶר, דְּטַעֲמָא דְּרַבָּנָן בְּאָשָׁם תָּלוּי כֵּיוָן דְּלִבּוֹ נוֹקְפוֹ גָּמַר וּמַקְדִּישׁ מִסָּפֵק, אֲבָל אָשָׁם וַדַּאי, כְּגוֹן אָמְרוּ לוֹ אָכַלְתָּ קֹדֶשׁ וְנוֹדַע שֶׁלֹּא חָטָא, שֶׁהוּזַמּוּ הָעֵדִים, אִי נַמִּי כְּסָבוּר קֹדֶשׁ אָכַל וְנִמְצָא חֻלִּין, אִגַּלַּאי מִלְּתָא דְּהֶקְדֵּשׁ טָעוּת הָיָה:


אשם ודאי אינו כן (an unconditional guilt-offering is not subject to the foregoing rule) – in this, the Rabbis do not dispute, for if he became aware before it (i.e., the animal) was slaughtered that he did not sin, it should go out to pasture among the flock, for the reason of the Rabbis regarding a suspending guilt-offering, since he may have scruples, he completed and dedicated it from doubt, but an unconditional guilt-offering, as, for example, if they said to him: “Did you eat Holy Things,” and it became known that he had not sinned when the witnesses were found to be plotting/lying, alternatively, when he thinks that everything is holy, but it is found to be unconsecrated, the matter is revealed that it was an erroneous consecration of property (and is not considered consecrated).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:2)


**הֲרֵי זֶה יִקָּבֵר.** כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא קַדִּישׁ הָוֵי כְּחֻלִּין שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטוּ בָּעֲזָרָה, דִּטְעוּנִין קְבוּרָה:


הרי זה יקבר – since it was not sanctified, it is like unconsecrated meat that was slaughtered in the Temple courtyard that requires burial.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:3)


**נִזְרַק הַדָּם הַבָּשָׂר יֵצֵא לְבֵית הַשְּׂרֵפָה.** בַּגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ דְּמִי שֶׁשָּׁנָה לְעֵיל הֲרֵי זֶה יִקָּבֵר לֹא שָׁנָה הָכָא הַבָּשָׂר יֵצֵא לְבֵית הַשְּׂרֵפָה, דְּכֵיוָן דְּסָבַר אָשָׁם וַדַּאי חֻלִּין הוּא וְלֹא קַדִּישׁ לְעוֹלָם, אֵינוֹ נִשְׂרָף אֶלָּא נִקְבָּר:


נזרק הדם הבשר יצא לבית השריפה – in the Gemara (Tractate Keritot 24b) that what was taught above (by the anonymous Mishnah) , “that this is to be buried,” was not taught here, that the meat should go forth to the place of burning, for since that he holds that an unconditional guilt-offering is unconsecrated [meat], and not holy forever, is not burned, but is buried.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:4)


**שׁוֹר הַנִּסְקָל אֵינוֹ כֵן.** כְּאָשָׁם תָּלוּי, דְּהָכָא לֹא פְּלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן שֶׁאִם נוֹדַע שֶׁלֹּא הָרַג יֵצֵא וְיִרְעֶה בָּעֵדֶר:


שור הנסקל אינו כן – like the suspending guilt-offering. For here, he Rabbis do not dispute that if he became aware/knowledgeable that it did not kill, it goes forth and pastures in the flock.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:5)


**עֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה אֵינָהּ כֵּן.** כְּאָשָׁם תָּלוּי, שֶׁאִם עַד שֶׁלֹּא נֶעֶרְפָה נִמְצָא הַהוֹרֵג, תֵּצֵא וְתִרְעֶה בָּעֵדֶר. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, שׁוֹר הַנִּסְקָל אֵינוֹ כֵן כְּאָשָׁם וַדַּאי, דְּאָשָׁם וַדַּאי מִשֶּׁנִּשְׁחַט הֲרֵי זֶה יִקָּבֵר, וְשׁוֹר הַנִּסְקָל כְּשֶׁנּוֹדַע לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּסְקַל מֻתָּר בַּהֲנָאָה. וְעֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה אֵינָהּ כֵּן כְּשׁוֹר הַנִּסְקָל, דְּשׁוֹר הַנִּסְקָל אִם נוֹדַע מִשֶּׁנִּסְקַל מֻתָּר בַּהֲנָאָה, וְעֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה אִם נוֹדַע הַהוֹרֵג מִשֶּׁנֶּעֶרְפָה הָעֶגְלָה תִּקָּבֵר בִּמְקוֹמָהּ:


עגלה ערופה אינו כן – like a suspending guilt-offering, for if before its neck was broken that the killer was found, it should sent forth and pastures in the flock. But Maimonides explained, that the bull/ox which is stoned is not subject to the foregoing rule like the unconditional guilt-offering, for an unconditional guilt-offering, once it is slaughtered, it should be buried, and the bull which is stoned, wen he became aware after it had been stoned, [it] is permissible for benefit. The heifer whose neck is broken, is not subject to the foregoing rule like the bull/ox that is stoned, for a bull/ox that is stoned, if [the owner] was made aware after it was stoned, [it] is permitted for benefit, and the heifer whose neck is broken, if he [the owner] is made aware of the killer [without whom, the neck of the heifer would not have been broken], it should be buried in its place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:2:6)


**כִּפְּרָה סְפֵקָהּ.** בִּשְׁעַת עֲרִיפָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי עֲדַיִן סָפֵק הָיָה, וְהָלְכָה לָהּ. הִלְכָּךְ אֲסוּרָה בַּהֲנָאָה, וְתִקָּבֵר כְּדִין כָּל הָעֶגְלוֹת הָעֲרוּפוֹת:


כיפר ספיקה (it made atonement for its matter of doubt) – at the time of the breaking of the neck, for there was yet a doubt, and it went on its way. Therefore, it is prohibited to benefit [from it], and all of the heifers with broken necks should be buried according to law.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:3:1)


**מִתְנַדֵּב אָדָם אָשָׁם תָּלוּי.** דְּכָל עַצְמוֹ שֶׁל אָשָׁם תָּלוּי נְדָבָה הוּא. דְּאִי סָלְקָא דַעְתָּךְ חוֹבָה הוּא, כִּי מִתְיְדַע לֵיהּ שֶׁחָטָא אַמַּאי מַיְתֵי חַטָּאת, אֶלָּא שְׁמַע מִנַּהּ נְדָבָה הוּא:


מתנדב אדם אשם תלוי – the essence of a suspensive guilt-offering is a free-will donation, for it you should think that it is obligatory, when he became conscious that he had sinned, why does he bring a sin-offering, but rather, we learn from it, that it is a free-will donation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:3:2)


**חוּץ מֵאַחַר יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים.** דִּשְׁאָר יְמוֹת הַשָּׁנָה הוּא דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ בִּסְפֵק שׁוּם חֵטְא שֶׁמָּא עָשָׂה אוֹ לָאו, אֲבָל הַשְׁתָּא לֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ, דְּהָא כִּפֵּר יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים:


חוץ מאחר יוה"כ – for the rest of the days of the year, where it is possible that suspect a doubt of some sin, lest he committed it or did not do so, but now, there isn’t any suspicion, because Yom Kippur atoned [for him].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:3:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אֵין מְבִיאִין אָשָׁם תָּלוּי.** דְּהַיְנוּ טַעֲמָא דְּמַיְתֵי אָשָׁם תָּלוּי מִקַּמֵּי דְּמִתְיְדַע לֵיהּ, לְהָגֵן עָלָיו מִן הַיִּסּוּרִים עַד שֶׁיִּוָּדַע לוֹ, שֶׁהַתּוֹרָה חָסָה עַל גּוּפוֹ שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאֵין אָשָׁם תָּלוּי בָּא בִּנְדָבָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים אין מביאין אשם תלוי – for that is the reason that he brings a suspensive guilt-offering before he becomes aware of it (i.e., whether he sinned or not), to protect him from the suffering until he becomes aware, for the Torah has compassion on the body of Israel, but the suspensive guilt-offering does not come as a free-will donation. But the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:4:1)


**חַיָּבִין לְהָבִיא לְאַחַר יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים.** דְּאָמַר קְרָא (ויקרא טז) מִכֹּל חַטֹּאתֵיכֶם לִפְנֵי ה'‏ תִּטְהָרוּ, חֵטְא שֶׁאֵין מַכִּיר בּוֹ אֶלָּא הַמָּקוֹם דְּהַיְנוּ שֶׁלֹּא נוֹדַע לוֹ שֶׁחָטָא, יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים מְכַפֵּר. אֲבָל חֵטְא דְּאִיכָּא דְּיָדַע בֵּיהּ חוּץ מִן הַמָּקוֹם, אֵין יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים מְכַפֵּר:


חייבין להביא לאחר יוה"כ – that Scripture states (Leviticus 16:30): “from all of your sins, you shall be pure before the LORD,” a sin that he doesn’t know about it other than God, meaning that he didn’t become aware that he had sinned, Yom Kippur atones, but a sin where there is an awareness of it except from God, Yom Kippur does not atone.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:5:1)


**חַטַּאת הָעוֹף סָפֵק.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁיָּלְדָה וְאֵין יוֹדְעִים אִם מִין חִיּוּב אִם מִין פְּטוּר:


חטאת העוף ספק – as for example, when she gave birth, and we don’t know if it is from a liable species [or] from an exempt species.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:5:2)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמַּכְשִׁירַתָּהּ לֶאֱכֹל בַּזְּבָחִים.** דִּמְחֻסֶּרֶת כַּפָּרָה הִיא מִסָּפֵק, וְאֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לֶאֱכֹל בַּקָּדָשִׁים עַד שֶׁתָּבִיא כַּפָּרָתָהּ:


מפני שמכשירתה לאכול בזבחים – since lacking atonement is from a doubt, and she cannot eat Holy Things until she brings her atonement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:5:3)


**מִשֶּׁנִּמְלְקָה נוֹדַע לָהּ.** שֶׁלֹּא יָלְדָה:


משנמלקה נודע לה – that she did not give birth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:5:4)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ תִקָּבֵר.** בְּדִין הוּא דְּמֻתֶּרֶת בַּהֲנָאָה, דְּחֻלִּין גְּמוּרִים הִיא, דְּמִשּׁוּם שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטָה בָּעֲזָרָה לֵיכָּא לְמֵיסַר, דְּלֹא אָסְרָה תּוֹרָה אֶלָּא שְׁחִיטָה, אֲבָל מְלִיקָה לֹא. אֲבָל רַבָּנָן גָּזְרוּ דַּאֲסוּרָה בַּהֲנָאָה שֶׁמָּא יֹאמְרוּ נֶהֱנִין מֵחַטַּאת הָעוֹף סָפֵק. וְחַטַּאת הָעוֹף הַבָּאָה עַל הַסָּפֵק אֵינָהּ נֶאֱכֶלֶת, דְּשֶׁמָּא חֻלִּין הִיא וּנְבֵלָה הִיא, דִּמְלִיקָה נְבֵלָה בְּחֻלִּין. וַאֲסוּרָה נַמִּי בַּהֲנָאָה, דְּשֶׁמָּא קֹדֶשׁ הִיא וְקֹדֶשׁ שֶׁאֵינוֹ נֶאֱכָל אָסוּר בַּהֲנָאָה:


הרי זו תקבר – by law that she is permitted to benefit, for it is completely unconsecrated, for because it was slaughtered in the Temple courtyard, one cannot prohibit it, for the Torah only prohibited ritual slaughter, but not the pinching of the neck [of the bird] with one’s fingernail. But the Rabbis decreed that she is prohibited to derive benefit, lest they say that we benefit from the sin offering of a fowl from doubt. But the sin-offering of a fowl that is brought on the doubt is not consumed, lest it is Holy and Holy Things that are not consumed are prohibited to derive benefit.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:1)


**הַמַּפְרִישׁ שְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים.** שֶׁכָּךְ הוּא דִּין אָשָׁם, דִּכְתִיב בַּאֲשַׁם מְעִילוֹת (שם ה) בְּעֶרְכְּךָ כֶּסֶף שְׁקָלִים בְּשֶׁקֶל הַקֹּדֶשׁ לְאָשָׁם, וְתַרְגּוּם שְׁקָלִים סְלָעִים. וַאֲשַׁם גְּזֵלוֹת וַאֲשַׁם תָּלוּי לְמֵדִים מֵאֲשַׁם מְעִילוֹת בִּגְזֵרָה שָׁוָה, נֶאֱמַר כָּאן בְּעֶרְכְּךָ, וְנֶאֱמַר לְהַלָּן בְּעֶרְכְּךָ. וַאֲשַׁם שִׁפְחָה חֲרוּפָה נַמִּי, הוֹאִיל וְהוּא אַיִל, הָוֵי אַף הוּא בְּכֶסֶף סְלָעִים כִּשְׁלֹשָׁה אֲשָׁמוֹת הַלָּלוּ שֶׁהֵן אַיִל. אֲבָל אֲשַׁם מְצֹרָע וַאֲשַׁם נָזִיר דִּבְתַרְוַיְהוּ כְּתִיב כֶּבֶשׂ לְאָשָׁם, אֵינָן בָּאִים בְּכֶסֶף שְׁקָלִים:


המפריש שתי סלעים – for such is the law regarding the reparation/guilt-offering (i.e., setting aside two Selaim), as it is written concerning the guilt-offering for religious sacrilege (Leviticus 5:15): “[When a person commits a trespass, being unwittingly remiss about any of the LORD’s sacred things, he shall bring as his penalty to the LORD a ram without blemish from the flock] convertible into payment to silver by the sanctuary weight, as a reparation offering/בערכך שקלים בשקל הקדש לאדם. And the Aramaic translation of Shekalim is Selaim. But the guilt-offering for theft and the suspensive guilt-offering are learned from the reparation/guilt-offering for religious sacrilege through a verbal analogy/Gezarah Shavah. It is stated here (Leviticus 5:15): "בערכך שקלים" /”convertible into payment,” and it is stated further on (Leviticus 5:18): “[He shall bring to the priest a ram without blemish from the flock] בערכך לאשם/or the equivalent, as a reparation offering. [The priest shall make expiation on his behalf for the error that he committed unwittingly, and he shall be forgiven],” and (Leviticus 5:25): “[Thus he shall bring to the priest, as his penalty to the LORD, a ram without blemish from the flock], or the equivalent, as a reparation offering/בערכך לאשם .” And the reparation offering for a designated maidservant also, for since it is a ram, it also with כסף סלעים /(at least) two silver Selaim – like these three guilt-offerings which are a ram, but the guilt-offering of a leper and the guilt-offering of a Nazir, in both of them, it is written כבש לאשם /a lamb as a guilt-offering (see Leviticus 14:21 and Numbers 6:12), they don’t come with כסף שקלים/silver Shekalim (plural, meaning a multiple of at least two).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:2)


**אִם הָיָה אַחַד מֵהֶם יָפֶה שְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבִּשְׁעַת הַפְרָשָׁה לֹא הָיָה יָפֶה אֶלָּא סֶלַע, אִם בִּשְׁעַת כַּפָּרָה יָפֶה שְׁתַּיִם, כָּשֵׁר, דִּבְמַה שֶּׁהוּא שָׁוֶה בִּשְׁעַת כַּפָּרָה אָזְלִינַן, הִלְכָּךְ אוֹתוֹ שֶׁהוּא יָפֶה עַכְשָׁיו בִּשְׁעַת כַּפָּרָה שְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים יִקְרַב לַאֲשָׁמוֹ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא קָנָהוּ מִתְּחִלָּה אֶלָּא בְּסֶלַע, וְהַשֵּׁנִי יִרְעֶה, לְפִי שֶׁנִּקַּח בִּמְעוֹת אָשָׁם וּלְשֵׁם אָשָׁם, וְיִפְּלוּ דָּמָיו לִנְדָבָה, דְּהָכִי קַיְמָא לָן מוֹתַר אָשָׁם לִנְדָבָה:


אם היה אחד מהם יפה שתי סלעים – even though that at the time of separation, it was not worth other than only a (i.e., one) Selah, if at the time of atonement if is worth “two” [Selaim or Shekalim], it is appropriate, for we follow according to what it is worth at the time of the atonement, therefore, that which is it worth now at the name of the atonement of two Selaim, he should offer for his guilt offering, but even though he had not acquired it at the outset other than for a Sela, and the second one should be put to pasture, because it was acquired with the monies for the guilt-offering and for the sake of the guilt offering, it’s monies should fall for a free-will donation, and this is what we hold, that the guilt-offering is permitted for a donation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:3)


**לָקַח בָּהֶם שְׁנֵי אֵילִים לְחֻלִּין.** לַאֲכִילָה, מָעַל בַּמָּעוֹת וְיָצְאוּ לְחֻלִּין:


לקח בהם שמי אלים לחולין – for eating, he has committed sacrilege with the monies and they have gone for unconsecrated animals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:4)


**יָפֶה עֲשָׂרָה זוּזִים.** כְּדֵי שְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים שֶׁמָּעַל בָּהֶן וְחֻמְשָׁן, שֶׁהַסֶּלַע אַרְבָּעָה זוּזִים:


יפה עשרה זוזים – in order that two Selaim that he committed religious sacrilege with them and their added fifth, for the Selah is four Zuzim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:5)


**הַיָּפֶה שְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים יִקְרַב לַאֲשָׁמוֹ.** לְשֵׁם אוֹתוֹ אָשָׁם שֶׁהִפְרִישׁ עָלָיו הַמָּעוֹת:


היפה שתי סלעים יקרב לאשמו – for the sake of that same reparation/guilt-offeirng that he separated the monies for.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:6)


**וְהַשֵּׁנִי לִמְעִילָתוֹ.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא, לֹא שֶׁיַּקְרִיב אוֹתוֹ לְאָשָׁם, אֶלָּא שֶׁיִּתְּנֶנּוּ לַגִּזְבָּר בִּשְׁבִיל שְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים שֶׁהוֹצִיא לְחֻלִּין, שֶׁנִּתְחַיֵּב בָּהֶן וּבְחֻמְשָׁן, שֶׁהֵם בֵּין הַקֶּרֶן וְהַחֹמֶשׁ עֲשָׂרָה זוּזִים. וְיָבִיא אָשָׁם בִּשְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים מִבֵּיתוֹ לְקָרְבַּן מְעִילָה:


והשני למעילתו – It explains in the Gemara (Tractate Keritot 26b) that he should not offer it for a guilt-offering, but rather, that he should give it to the treasurer for the two Selaim that he removed for unconsecrated animals , that he was liable for them and their added firth, which are between the principal and the [added] fifth ten Zuzim, and he should bring a guilt-offering of two Selaim from his house for the sacrifice of religious sacrilege.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:7)


**אֶחָד לְאָשָׁם וְאֶחָד לְחֻלִּין.** לָקַח בִּשְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים שֶׁהִפְרִישׁ שֶׁהָיוּ קֹדֶשׁ, שְׁנֵי אֵילִים אֶחָד לְאָשָׁם וְאֶחָד לַאֲכִילַת חֻלִּין, וּמָעַל בְּסֶלַע:


אחד לאשם ואחד לחולין – he purchased with the two Selaim that he separated that were Holy two rams – one for a guilt offering and the other for consuming unconsecrated food, for he had committed sacrilege with one Sela.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:8)


**אִם הָיָה שֶׁל אָשָׁם יָפֶה שְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים יִקְרַב לַאֲשָׁמוֹ.** הָרִאשׁוֹן, שֶׁהֲרֵי לִשְׁמוֹ נִלְקַח מִמָּעוֹת שֶׁנִּפְרְשׁוּ לוֹ:


אם היה של אשם יפה שתי סלעים יקרב לאשמו – the first one (i.e., the guilt offering that he is liable for), is purchased from the monies separated for this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:9)


**וְהַשֵּׁנִי.** שֶׁהוּא חֻלִּין, יִקְרַב לִמְעִילָתוֹ לַאֲשַׁם מְעִילוֹת עַל שֶׁהוֹצִיא סֶלַע אֶחָד שֶׁל קֹדֶשׁ לְחֻלִּין. וּכְגוֹן דְּאִיהוּ נַמִּי יָפֶה שְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים, דְּאֵין אֲשַׁם מְעִילוֹת פָּחוּת מִשְּׁתֵּי סְלָעִים כֶּסֶף:


והשני – which is unconsecrated, will be offered for his sacrilege, for the reparation offering of his religious sacrilege in that he spent one Selah [that had been dedicated] of the Holy [Things] for unconsecrated things, and, as for example, that I was also worth two Selahs, for the guilt-offering for sacrileges is not less than two silver Selahs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:6:10)


**וְיָבִיא עִמָּהּ סֶלַע וְחֻמְשָׁהּ.** קֶרֶן שֶׁמָּעַל בּוֹ וְחֻמְשָׁהּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי סֶלַע הַקֹּדֶשׁ הוֹצִיא לְחֻלִּין:


עמה סלע וחומשה – the principal for which he committed sacrilege and its [added] fifth, for he spent a Selah of [money devoted to] the Holy for unconsecrated things.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:7:1)


**לֹא יְבִיאֶנָּה בְנוֹ אַחֲרָיו.** אִם שָׁגַג הַבֵּן בְּשִׁגְגַת חַטָּאת, לֹא יָבִיא חַטָּאת שֶׁהִפְרִישׁ אָבִיו כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּכֻפַּר בָּהּ עַל שִׁגְגָתוֹ:


לא יביאנה בנו אחריו – if the son sinned unwittingly with the unwitting sin-offering, he should not bring a sin-offering that his father had separated in order through it, he will achieve atonement on his inadvertent act.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:1)


**מְבִיאִים מֵהֶקְדֵּשׁ כִּשְׂבָּה, שְׂעִירָה.** אִם הִפְרִישׁ מָעוֹת לִקַּח כִּשְׂבָּה לְחַטָּאתוֹ, יָכוֹל לִקַּח בָּהֶן שְׂעִירָה אִם יִרְצֶה:


מביאים המקדש כשבה שעירה – if he separated money to purchase a female lamb for his sin-offering, he is able to procure with them a female goat if he wishes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:2)


**הִפְרִישׁ לְכִשְׂבָּה אוֹ לִשְׂעִירָה.** הִפְרִישׁ מָעוֹת לִקְנוֹת בָּהֶן כִּשְׂבָּה אוֹ שְׂעִירָה:


הפריש לכשבה או לשעירה – he separated money to purchase with them a female lamb or a female goat.’


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:3)


**הֶעֱנִי.** יָבִיא בָּהֶן עוֹף, וְהַשְּׁאָר חֻלִּין. דִּכְתִיב בְּקָרְבַּן עוֹלֶה וְיוֹרֵד גַּבֵּי כִּשְׂבָּה וּשְׂעִירָה, מֵחַטָּאתוֹ אֲשֶׁר חָטָא, דְּמַשְׁמַע מִמִּקְצָת הַדָּבָר שֶׁהִפְרִישׁ לְחַטָּאתוֹ, שֶׁאִם הֶעֱנִי יָבִיא עוֹף מִמִּקְצָת דָּמִים הַלָּלוּ. וְגַבֵּי עוֹף נַמִּי כְּתִיב מֵחַטָּאתוֹ, דְּמַשְׁמַע דְּאִם הֶעֱנִי יוֹתֵר מֵבִיא מִמִּקְצָת דְּמֵי הָעוֹף עֲשִׂירִית הָאֵיפָה. וְגַבֵּי עֲשִׂירִית הָאֵיפָה כְּתִיב עַל חַטָּאתוֹ, דְּמַשְׁמַע אִם הֶעֱשִׁיר יוֹסִיף עַל הַדָּמִים הַלָּלוּ וְיָבִיא עוֹף, אוֹ אִם הֶעֱשִׁיר יוֹתֵר יָבִיא כִּשְׂבָּה אוֹ שְׂעִירָה:


העני – [if he became poor] he should bring with them a bird, and the rest is unconsecrated, as it is written for a sacrifice on a sliding scale (i.e., depending upon financial ability), concerning a female lamb or female goat ((Leviticus 5:10): “[And the second he shall prepare as a burnt offering, according to regulation. Thus shall the priest make expiation on his behalf] for the sin of which he is guilty,” which implies that from part of the thing that he separated for his sin-offering, for should he become poor, he would bring fowl from a part of these moneys. And concerning a bird, also, it is written "מחטאתו" /for the sin (see Leviticus 5:6 – “and the priest shall make expiation on his behalf for his sin.”), implying that if he became more destitute, he brings from part of the monies of the bird a tenth of an ephah; and concerning the tenth of an ephah, it is written, "על חטאתו"/for whichever of these sins” (Leviticus 5:13), which implies that if he becomes more wealthy, he should add to these monies and bring a bird, or if he became more wealth, he should bring a female lamb or a female goat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:4)


**וְנִסְתָּאֲבָה.** הוּמְמָה:


ונסתאבה – it became defective.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:5)


**אִם רָצָה יָבִיא בְדָמֶיהָ עוֹף.** כְּגוֹן אִם הֶעֱנִי:


אם רצה יביא בדמיהן עוף – as for example, if he became poor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:8:6)


**שֶׁאֵין לָעוֹף פִּדְיוֹן.** דְּבִפְסוּלֵי הַמֻּקְדָּשִׁים כְּתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְהֶעֱמִיד אֶת הַבְּהֵמָה לִפְנֵי הַכֹּהֵן, וְהָיָה אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיֹּאמַר וְהֶעֱמִיד אוֹתָהּ, מַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר הַבְּהֵמָה, לוֹמַר לָךְ בְּהֵמָה נִפְדֵּית וְאֵין עוֹפוֹת וְלֹא עֵצִים וְלֹא לְבוֹנָה וְלֹא כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת נִפְדִּים:


שאין לעוף פדיון – it is written regarding consecrated animals that are disqualified (Leviticus 27:11): “[if (the vow concerns) any impure animal that may not be brought as an offering to the LORD] the animal shall be presented before the priest,” and it was possible that it (i.e., the Torah) should have said, "והעמיד אותה"/and he would present it – what does the inference teach us when it says, "הבהמה" /the animal (in this verse)? To teach you that an animal is redeemed but not birds nor wood, nor frankincense and neither the service vessels [of the Temple] are redeemed.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:9:1)


**כְּבָשִׂים קוֹדְמִים לָעִזִּים.** בְּכָל דּוּכְתָּא הִקְדִּים הַכָּתוּב כְּבָשִׂים לָעִזִּים, דִּכְתִיב (שמות יב) מִן הַכְּבָשִׂים וּמִן הָעִזִּים תִּקָּחוּ, וְכֵן (במדבר טו) אוֹ לַשֶּׂה בַכְּבָשִׂים אוֹ בָעִזִּים. יָכוֹל, הָאוֹמֵר הֲרֵי עָלַי עוֹלָה וְיֵשׁ לוֹ כֶּבֶשׂ וְעֵז יָבִיא כֶּבֶשׂ דַּוְקָא:


כבשים קודמים לעזים – in every place, Scripture advanced lambs t goats, as it is written (Exodus 12:5): “[Your lamb shall be without blemish, a yearling male;] you may take it from the sheep or from the goats,” and similarly, (Numbers 15:11): “[Thus shall be done with each ox, with each ram,] and with any sheep or goat.” It is possible that the person who says, “This burnt-offering is upon me,” and he has and he has a male lamb or a goat, he should bring the lamb, specifically.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:9:2)


**תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וְאִם כֶּבֶשׂ יָבִיא קָרְבָּנוֹ.** וּלְעֵיל מִנֵּיהּ כְּתִיב וְהֵבִיא אֶת קָרְבָּנוֹ שְׂעִירַת עִזִּים, כָּאן הִקְדִּים עֵז לַכֶּבֶשׂ, לְלַמֵּד שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם שְׁקוּלִים וְאֵי זֶה מֵהֶן שֶׁיִּרְצֶה יָבִיא:


תלמוד לומר ואם כבש יביא קרבנו – but above this it is written (Leviticus 4:23): “[or the sin of which he is guilty is brought to his knowledge] –he shall bring as his offering a male goat without blemish;” here, it (i.e., the Torah) advanced the goat to the lamb, to teach that both are equivalent and that he may bring either of them that he wishes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:9:3)


**תּוֹרִים קוֹדְמִין לִבְנֵי יוֹנָה.** דִּבְרֹב מְקוֹמוֹת כְּתִיב בְּרֵישָׁא תּוֹרִים וַהֲדַר בְּנֵי יוֹנָה:


תורים קודמין לבני יונה – for in most places, turtle-doves are written first and afterwards pigeons.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:9:4)


**אִם זָכָה הַבֵּן לִפְנֵי הָרַב.** שֶׁרֹב חָכְמָתוֹ לָמַד מִמֶּנּוּ:


אם זכה הבן לפני הבן – that most of his wisdom he learned from him (see Tractate Bava Metzia, Chapter 2, Mishnah 11 for a parallel statement).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Keritot 6:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Keritot/r/6:9:5)


**קוֹדֵם אֶת הָאָב.** בְּכָל מָקוֹם, כְּגוֹן לְהָשִׁיב אֲבֵדָה וְלִפְדּוֹת מִבֵּית הַשֶּׁבִי וּלְהַחֲיוֹת וְלִפְרֹק עִמּוֹ. וְאִם אָבִיו תַּלְמִיד חָכָם אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ שָׁקוּל כְּנֶגֶד רַבּוֹ, אָבִיו קוֹדֵם לְרַבּוֹ, וַאֲפִלּוּ רַבּוֹ מֻבְהָק:


קודם את האב בכל מקום – as for example, to restore a lost object and to redeem from captivity, and to preserve life and to unload [an animal] with him. But if his father is a scholar, even though he is not equivalent corresponding to his teacher, his father comes before his teacher/Rabbi , and even his distinguished teacher [who is a scholar].
